/* <![CDATA[ (head-cquotient.js) */
var CQuotient = window.CQuotient = {};
CQuotient.clientId = 'bcmf-Dunhams';
CQuotient.realm = 'BCMF';
CQuotient.siteId = 'Dunhams';
CQuotient.instanceType = 'prd';
CQuotient.activities = [];
CQuotient.cqcid='';
CQuotient.cquid='';
CQuotient.initFromCookies = function () {
	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
	for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
	  var c = ca[i];
	  while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
	  if (c.indexOf('cqcid=') == 0) {
		  CQuotient.cqcid=c.substring('cqcid='.length,c.length);
	  } else if (c.indexOf('cquid=') == 0) {
		  CQuotient.cquid=c.substring('cquid='.length,c.length);
		  break;
	  }
	}
}
CQuotient.getCQCookieId = function () {
	if(window.CQuotient.cqcid == '')
		window.CQuotient.initFromCookies();
	return window.CQuotient.cqcid;
};
CQuotient.getCQUserId = function () {
	if(window.CQuotient.cquid == '')
		window.CQuotient.initFromCookies();
	return window.CQuotient.cquid;
};
CQuotient.trackEventsFromAC = function (/* Object or Array */ events) {
try {
	if (Object.prototype.toString.call(events) === "[object Array]") {
		events.forEach(_trackASingleCQEvent);
	} else {
		CQuotient._trackASingleCQEvent(events);
	}
} catch(err) {}
};
CQuotient._trackASingleCQEvent = function ( /* Object */ event) {
	if (event && event.id) {
		if (event.type === dw.ac.EV_PRD_DETAIL) {
			CQuotient.trackViewProduct( {id:'', alt_id: event.id, type: 'raw_sku'} );
		} // not handling the other dw.ac.* events currently
	}
};
CQuotient.trackViewProduct = function(/* Object */ cqParamData){
	var cq_params = {};
	cq_params.cookieId = CQuotient.getCQCookieId();
	cq_params.userId = CQuotient.getCQUserId();
	cq_params.product = cqParamData.product;
	cq_params.realm = cqParamData.realm;
	cq_params.siteId = cqParamData.siteId;
	cq_params.instanceType = cqParamData.instanceType;
	
	if(CQuotient.sendActivity) {
		CQuotient.sendActivity(CQuotient.clientId, 'viewProduct', cq_params);
	} else {
		CQuotient.activities.push({activityType: 'viewProduct', parameters: cq_params});
	}
};
/* ]]> */

<script src="https://ajax.googleapis.com/ajax/libs/jquery/1.9.1/jquery.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
/* NEW PRINT COUPON FUNCTIONALITY - BEGIN */
var mainprodimgsrc="";
var mainprodname="";
var mainprodprice="";
var mainprodsalesprice="";
var mainprodbarcodesvg="";
var mainproddesc="";
var popupvalout="";

$(document).ready(function(){
    updateWeeklyAdLinks();
    reWritePrint();
});

$(window).on("load", chStoreLoc);
//$(window).load(function(){
//    chStoreLoc();
//});
function chStoreLoc(){
    var hpurl=""+window.location.href;
    if(hpurl.indexOf("#store-locations")!=-1){
        var linktxt=$("a.button-storelocator-full").html();
        $("a.button-storelocator-full").click();
    }
}
function updateWeeklyAdLinks(){
    var stid2chtemp="";
    var stid2ch=$(".storelocator-wrapper").attr("data-store");
    if(stid2ch.substr(0,2)=="00"){
        stid2chtemp=stid2ch.substr(2,1);
    }else if(stid2ch.substr(0,1)=="0"){
        stid2chtemp=stid2ch.substr(1,2);
    }else{
        stid2chtemp=stid2ch;
    }
    $('.navigation-link').each(function(i, obj){
        if($(obj).html().indexOf("Weekly Ads")!=-1){
            $(obj).attr("href", "https://dunhamssports.inserts2online.com/storeReview.jsp?drpStoreID="+stid2chtemp);
        }
    });
    $('.footer-navigation-link').each(function(i, obj){
        if($(obj).html().indexOf("Weekly Ads")!=-1){
            $(obj).attr("href", "https://dunhamssports.inserts2online.com/storeReview.jsp?drpStoreID="+stid2chtemp);
        }
    });
}

function reWritePrint(){
    var link2rep='window.print()';
    var childval='buildPopupOutput()';
    var curcouponhtml=$(".coupon-wrap").html();
    if(curcouponhtml){
        //check if the html contains link2rep before continuing
        //if not found, exit - if found, continue function
        if(curcouponhtml.indexOf(link2rep)!=-1){
            newcouponval=curcouponhtml.replace(link2rep, childval);
            //write out new print coupon link
            $(".coupon-wrap").html(newcouponval);
            //if($("#primary")){
            //    $("#primary").append(msgdivval);
            //}
        }
        //newcouponval=curcouponhtml.replace(link2rep, childval);
        //write out new print coupon link
        //$(".coupon-wrap").html(newcouponval);
    }else{
        return false;
    }
}

function buildPopupOutput(){
/* - GET EACH ELEMENT INTO A VAR */
/* main image - width 200px; class of image: .primary-image */
mainprodimgsrc=$("img.primary-image").attr("src");
/* get the header / item name */
mainprodname=$("h1.product-name").html();
/* get the reg price and sale price */
mainprodprice=$("span.price-standard").html();
mainprodsalesprice=$("span.product-sales-price").html();
/* coupon code / bar code img */
mainprodbarcodesvg=$("div.coupon").html();
/* item description */
mainproddesc=$("#tab-1 > div.product-details").html();
/* - REBUILD VARS, OPEN POPUP AND SEND TO POPUP */
popupvalout="";
popupvalout+="Product Name: "+mainprodname+"</br>";
popupvalout+="Price: "+mainprodprice+"</br>";
popupvalout+="Sale Price: "+mainprodsalesprice+"</br>";
popupvalout+="Coupon Code: </br>"+mainprodbarcodesvg+"</br>";
popupvalout+="<img src='"+mainprodimgsrc+"' width='200px'></br>"
/* - CALL THE POPUP */
openAndPrintPopup(popupvalout);
}

function openAndPrintPopup(dataelem)
{
    var datatopopup=dataelem;
    if(datatopopup!=""){
        var mywindow = window.open('', 'new div', 'height=400,width=600');
        mywindow.document.write('<html><head><title>Dunham&apos;s Print</title>');
        mywindow.document.write('</head><body ><div style="max-width:500px;">');
        mywindow.document.write(datatopopup);
        mywindow.document.write('</div></body></html>');
        mywindow.print();
        mywindow.close();
        return true;
    }
}
/* NEW PRINT COUPON FUNCTIONALITY - END */

                }else if(cururl.indexOf("/by-sport/")!=-1){
                    $("#primary").prepend(msgdivval);

                    $("#primary").prepend(msgdivval);

        }

function chStoreModalProdPage(){
    if(onprodpage=="yes"){
        //find span with class product-availability-store and add link to the store city state
        html2mod=$("span.product-availability-store").html();
        //check if javascript:trigrStorModal has indexOf in html before updating
        if(html2mod.indexOf("javascript:trigrStorModal")==-1){
            h2marr=html2mod.split(")");
            h2mtemp=h2marr[0].replace("(Store Selected = ", "");
            h2m2pass="(Store Selected = <a href='javascript:trigrStorModal()'>"+h2mtemp+"</a>)"+h2marr[1];
            $("span.product-availability-store").html(h2m2pass);
        }
    }
}
function trigrStorModal(){
    //alert("in");
    $("a.home-storelocator").click();
}

$(document).ready(function(){
    MutationObserver = window.MutationObserver || window.WebKitMutationObserver;
    var observer = new MutationObserver(function(mutations, observer) {
        // fired when a mutation occurs
        //console.log(mutations, observer);
        reInitFuncs();
        // ...
    });
    // define what element should be observed by the observer
    // and what types of mutations trigger the callback
    observer.observe(document, {
      subtree: true,
      attributes: true
      //...
    });
});

function reInitFuncs(){
    reWritePrint();
    chStoreMsgDiv();
    chStoreModalProdPage();
    chReWriteImgZoom();
}

<script src="https://ajax.googleapis.com/ajax/libs/jquery/1.9.1/jquery.min.js" type="text/javascript"></script>
<script type="text/javascript">
;(function($){
    $.fn.simpleZoom = function(options){
        var defs = {
            zoomBox : "#zoomBox",
            markSize : [200, 100],
            zoomSize : [400, 400],
            zoomImg : [800, 800]
        };
        var opt = $.fn.extend({}, defs, options);
        return this.each(function(){
            var markBox = $(this);
            var zoomBox = $(opt.zoomBox);
            var zoom_img = $(opt.zoomBox).find("img"); 
            var markBoxSize = [markBox.width(), markBox.height(), markBox.offset().left, markBox.offset().top];
            var markSize = opt.markSize;
            var zoomSize = opt.zoomSize;
            var zoomImg = opt.zoomImg;
            var mark_ele = "<i id='mark'></i>";
            var mark_css = {position:"absolute", top:0, left:0, width:markSize[0]+"px", height:markSize[1]+"px", backgroundColor:"#000", opacity:.5, filter:"alpha(opacity=50)",  display:"none", cursor:"crosshair"};
            
            var show_w = markBoxSize[0]-markSize[0];
            var show_h = markBoxSize[1]-markSize[1];

            //created mark element and add cascading style sheets
            zoomBox.css({width:zoomSize[0]+"px", height:zoomSize[1]+"px"});
            markBox.append(mark_ele);
            $("#mark").css(mark_css);

            markBox.mouseover(function(){
                $("#mark").show();
                zoomBox.show();
            }).mouseout(function(){
                $("#mark").hide();
                zoomBox.hide();
            }).mousemove(function(e){
                var l = e.pageX-markBoxSize[2]-(markSize[0]/2);
                var t = e.pageY-markBoxSize[3]-(markSize[1]/2);
                if(l < 0){
                    l = 0;
                }else if(l > show_w){
                    l = show_w;
                }
                if(t < 0){
                    t = 0;
                }else if(t > show_h){
                    t = show_h;
                }

                $("#mark").css({left:l+"px", top:t+"px"});
                
                var z_x = -(l/show_w)*(zoomImg[0]-zoomSize[0]);
                var z_y = -(t/show_h)*(zoomImg[1]-zoomSize[1]);
                zoom_img.css({left:z_x+"px", top:z_y+"px"});
            });
        });
    }
})(jQuery);
//$.noConflict();
var j = jQuery.noConflict();
var curprodimg="";
var zoomHTML="";
var prodprimeimgdiv="";
var ppidiv="";
//maybe execute on window load - not doc ready?
j(document).ready(function(){
    //call this func on mutation when image gets changed
    reWriteImgZoom();
});
function chReWriteImgZoom(){
    /* on mutation, check if the image zoom needs to be recreated by comparing html of primary-image */
    //alert(j("#zoomdyna").html());
    if(j("#zoomdyna").html()==undefined){
        reWriteImgZoom();
    }
}
function reWriteImgZoom(){
    var maindivw=j("#main").width();
    if(maindivw>1182 && onprodpage=="yes"){
        //CHECK IF ON PRODUCT DETAIL PAGE: /product/ IN URL, ETC.
        curprodimg="";
        curprodimg=j(".primary-image").attr("src");
        if(curprodimg){
            zoomHTML='';
            zoomHTML+='<div class="main">';
            zoomHTML+='  <div id="showdyna"><img class="primary-image" src="'+curprodimg+'" id="show_pic" width="350" height="350"/></div>';
            zoomHTML+='  <div id="zoomdyna"><img class="primary-image" src="'+curprodimg+'" style="width:800px; height:800px;" width="800" height="800"/></div>';
            zoomHTML+='</div>';
            //before rewriting the html of the product-primary-image for the first time
            //set a var's val to hold it for future use so that we can then check if it has 
            //value then just add the zoomHTML on top of it
            if(prodprimeimgdiv==""){
                prodprimeimgdiv=j(".product-primary-image").html();
            }
            /* check the width of .product-primary-image is not less than 350px, if it is then
            check if it needs to be rewritten back to initial mobile-version of img, non-imageZoom html
            which was set above to the prodprimeimgdiv var */
            j(".product-primary-image").html(zoomHTML);    
            //alert(curprodimg);
            j("#showdyna").simpleZoom({
                zoomBox:"#zoomdyna",
                markSize:[150, 150],
                zoomSize:[350, 350],
                zoomImg:[800, 800]
            });
        }
    }
}

<script type="text/javascript">
/* GLOBAL VARS */
//get the current url:
var cururl=""+window.location.href;
/* INIT */
$(document).ready(function(){
    chStoreSchema();
});
/* FUNCTIONS */
function chStoreSchema(){
    //check if on store page - look for StoreID
    if(cururl.indexOf('/Stores-Details')!=-1){
        var storid4schema=getParameterByName('storeid',cururl.toLowerCase());
        if(storid4schema){
            //alert("Build Schema for Store ID: "+storid4schema);
            //build store schema and send to header:
            buildStoreSchema(storid4schema);
        }
    }
}
function buildStoreSchema(wh){
    var phtemp="";
    var addr1temp="";
    var addr2temp="";
    var cittemp="";
    var stntemp="";
    var zctemp="";
    var suhtemp="";
    var mohtemp="";
    var tuhtemp="";
    var wehtemp="";
    var thhtemp="";
    var frhtemp="";
    var sahtemp="";
    //loop through ssobjv2 and find matching id
    for(i=0;i<ssobjv2.length;i++){
        if(ssobjv2[i].id==wh){
            whid=i;
            phtemp=ssobjv2[i].ph;
            addr1temp=ssobjv2[i].addr1;
            addr2temp=ssobjv2[i].addr2;
            cittemp=ssobjv2[i].cit;
            stntemp=ssobjv2[i].stn;
            zctemp=ssobjv2[i].zc;
            suhtemp=ssobjv2[i].suh;
            mohtemp=ssobjv2[i].moh;
            tuhtemp=ssobjv2[i].tuh;
            wehtemp=ssobjv2[i].weh;
            thhtemp=ssobjv2[i].thh;
            frhtemp=ssobjv2[i].frh;
            sahtemp=ssobjv2[i].sah;
        }
    }
    //build out schema and append to head:
    //https://production-na01-dunhams.demandware.net/on/demandware.static/-/Library-Sites-DunhamSharedLibrary/default/dw28c8694b/images/logo.svg
    var name2pass="Sporting Goods in "+cittemp+", "+stntemp;    
    var addr2pass=""+addr1temp;
    var mohtemparr=mohtemp.split("|");
    var mohopen2pass="2015-07-27T"+mohtemparr[0];
    var mohclos2pass="2015-07-27T"+mohtemparr[1];
    var sahtemparr=sahtemp.split("|");
    var sahopen2pass="2015-07-27T"+sahtemparr[0];
    var sahclos2pass="2015-07-27T"+sahtemparr[1];
    var suhtemparr=suhtemp.split("|");
    var suhopen2pass="2015-07-27T"+suhtemparr[0];
    var suhclos2pass="2015-07-27T"+suhtemparr[1];
    if(addr2temp!=""){
        addr2pass+=" "+addr2temp;
    }
    var storschemadata={
      "@context" : "http://schema.org",
      "@type" : "LocalBusiness",
      "name" : name2pass,
      "image" : "https://www.dunhamssports.com/on/demandware.static/-/Library-Sites-DunhamSharedLibrary/default/dw28c8694b/images/logo.svg",
      "telephone" : phtemp,
      "address" : {
        "@type" : "PostalAddress",
        "streetAddress" : addr2pass,
        "addressLocality" : cittemp,
        "addressRegion" : stntemp,
        "postalCode" : zctemp
      },
      "openingHoursSpecification" : [ {
        "@type" : "OpeningHoursSpecification",
        "dayOfWeek" : {
          "@type" : "DayOfWeek",
          "name" : "Mon-Fri"
        },
        "opens" : mohopen2pass,
        "closes" : mohclos2pass
      }, {
        "@type" : "OpeningHoursSpecification",
        "dayOfWeek" : {
          "@type" : "DayOfWeek",
          "name" : "Sat"
        },
        "opens" : sahopen2pass,
        "closes" : sahclos2pass
      }, {
        "@type" : "OpeningHoursSpecification",
        "dayOfWeek" : {
          "@type" : "DayOfWeek",
          "name" : "Sun"
        },
        "opens" : suhopen2pass,
        "closes" : suhclos2pass
      } ]
    };
    //creating the script element and storing the JSON-LD
    var storscript = document.createElement('script');
    storscript.type = "application/ld+json";
    storscript.innerHTML = JSON.stringify(storschemadata);
    document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0].appendChild(storscript);
}
function getParameterByName(name, url){
    if(!url){
        url=window.location.href;
        }
    name=name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, '\\$&');
    var regex=new RegExp('[?&]'+name+'(=([^&#]*)|&|#|$)'),results=regex.exec(url);
    if(!results){
        return null;
        }
    if(!results[2]){
        return '';
        }
    return decodeURIComponent(results[2].replace(/\+/g, ' '));
}

</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
